```text
Now I have all the data I need. Let me compile the analysis:

**exp_code_qwen1b5_pythia_scorer (Pythia-1.4B scorer):**
- K* = 2
- AUC 0v1 = 0.9076
- AUC 1v2 = 0.7197
- AUC 2v3 = 0.5308
- AUC 3v4 = 0.5236


- AUC 4v5 = 0.5084
- JSD 0v1 = 0.065423
- JSD 1v2 = 0.035809
- JSD 2v3 = 0.004315
- JSD 3v4 = 0.003182
- JSD 4v5 = 0.003047
- Ordinal MAE = 1.2578
- Ordinal Accuracy = 0.3433

**exp_code_qwen1b5 (Qwen-1.5B scorer, original):**
- K* = 2
- AUC 0v1 = 0.9388

The Pythia scorer shows strong discrimination at the lower end but degrades significantly for higher-level comparisons, with JSD values dropping sharply after the first comparison. The original Qwen model starts with an even stronger AUC on the 0v1 split. Now I'm looking at the performance metrics across these tools. The AUC differences between models are minimal—dropping just 2-3% at the strongest discrimination points, which makes sense given that Pythia is the weaker model. What's striking is that despite these small variations, the optimal K* stays fixed at 2, suggesting the boundary is robust across different feature extractors. This aligns with what I'd expect from cross-scorer validation on arXiv data.

Now I need to update the registry with these findings.
```

Registry 已更新。分析总结：

## exp_code_qwen1b5_pythia_scorer 结果

**K\* = 2，与 Qwen self-scorer 一致，确认 Code domain K\*=2 是 scorer-independent。**

### AUC 对比（Qwen scorer → Pythia scorer）

| Pair | Qwen scorer | Pythia scorer | Δ |
|------|-------------|---------------|---|
| 0v1 | 0.9388 | 0.9076 | -0.031 |
| 1v2 | 0.7382 | 0.7197 | -0.019 |
| 2v3 | 0.5300 | 0.5308 | +0.001 |
| 3v4 | 0.5022 | 0.5236 | +0.021 |
| 4v5 | 0.5219 | 0.5084 | -0.014 |

Pythia 在 discriminable 区间（0v1, 1v2）AUC 略低 2~3%（Pythia 1.4B 特征提取能力稍弱于 Qwen 1.5B），但 K\* 边界处（1v2→2v3）的跳变完全一致：1v2 远高于 θ=0.6，2v3 远低于 θ。JSD 也呈相同模式，depth 2→3 处急剧坍缩（0.0358→0.0043）。

### 对 domain ceiling 理论的支撑

1. **Code K\*=2 text-intrinsic**：换 scorer 后 K\* 不变，排除 Qwen family bias 导致的虚高。
2. **与 arXiv 一致的 pattern**：arXiv LLaMA-8B/Mistral-7B cross-scorer 也确认 K\*=3 不变。跨域、跨 scorer 验证均通过。
3. **Domain complexity hierarchy 稳固**：C4/Code K\*=2 < arXiv K\*=3，这个排序不受 scorer 选择影响，是文本本身结构复杂度的反映。
